				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:7} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-7 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-7}

			Who goeth a warfare any time at his own charges?  who planteth a vineyard, and eateth not of the fruit thereof?  or who feedeth a flock, and eateth not of the milk of the flock?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τίς στρατεύεται ἰδίοις ὀψωνίοις ποτέ; τίς φυτεύει ἀμπελῶνα καὶ τὸν καρπὸν αὐτοῦ οὐκ ἐσθίει; τίς ποιμαίνει ποίμνην καὶ ἐκ τοῦ γάλακτος τῆς ποίμνης οὐκ ἐσθίει;}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:8} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-8 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-8}

			Say I these things as a man?  or saith not the law the same also?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μὴ κατὰ ἄνθρωπον ταῦτα λαλῶ ἢ καὶ ὁ νόμος ταῦτα οὐ λέγει;}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:9} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-9 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-9}

			For it is written in the law of Moses, Thou shalt not muzzle the mouth of the ox that treadeth out the corn.  Doth God take care for oxen?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν γὰρ τῷ Μωϋσέως νόμῳ γέγραπται· Οὐ κημώσεις βοῦν ἀλοῶντα. μὴ τῶν βοῶν μέλει τῷ θεῷ,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:10} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-10 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-10}

			Or saith he it altogether for our sakes?  For our sakes, no doubt, this is written: that he that ploweth should plow in hope; and that he that thresheth in hope should be partaker of his hope.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἢ δι᾽ ἡμᾶς πάντως λέγει; δι᾽ ἡμᾶς γὰρ ἐγράφη, ὅτι ὀφείλει ἐπ᾽ ἐλπίδι ὁ ἀροτριῶν ἀροτριᾶν, καὶ ὁ ἀλοῶν ἐπ᾽ ἐλπίδι τοῦ μετέχειν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:11} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-11 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-11}

			If we have sown unto you spiritual things, is it a great thing if we shall reap your carnal things?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ ἡμεῖς ὑμῖν τὰ πνευματικὰ ἐσπείραμεν, μέγα εἰ ἡμεῖς ὑμῶν τὰ σαρκικὰ θερίσομεν;}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:12} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-12 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-12}

			If others be partakers of this power over you, are not we rather?  Nevertheless we have not used this power; but suffer all things, lest we should hinder the gospel of Christ.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ ἄλλοι τῆς ὑμῶν ἐξουσίας μετέχουσιν, οὐ μᾶλλον ἡμεῖς;
					Ἀλλ᾽ οὐκ ἐχρησάμεθα τῇ ἐξουσίᾳ ταύτῃ, ἀλλὰ πάντα στέγομεν ἵνα μή τινα ἐγκοπὴν δῶμεν τῷ εὐαγγελίῳ τοῦ Χριστοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:13} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-13 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-13}

			Do ye not know that they which minister about holy things live of the things of the temple?  and they which wait at the altar are partakers with the altar?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐκ οἴδατε ὅτι οἱ τὰ ἱερὰ ἐργαζόμενοι τὰ ἐκ τοῦ ἱεροῦ ἐσθίουσιν, οἱ τῷ θυσιαστηρίῳ παρεδρεύοντες τῷ θυσιαστηρίῳ συμμερίζονται;}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:14} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-14 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-14}

			Even so hath the Lord ordained that they which preach the gospel should live of the gospel.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὕτως καὶ ὁ κύριος διέταξεν τοῖς τὸ εὐαγγέλιον καταγγέλλουσιν ἐκ τοῦ εὐαγγελίου ζῆν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:15} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-15 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-15}

			But I have used none of these things: neither have I written these things, that it should be so done unto me: for it were better for me to die, than that any man should make my glorying void.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐγὼ δὲ οὐ κέχρημαι οὐδενὶ τούτων. οὐκ ἔγραψα δὲ ταῦτα ἵνα οὕτως γένηται ἐν ἐμοί, καλὸν γάρ μοι μᾶλλον ἀποθανεῖν ἤ— τὸ καύχημά μου οὐδεὶς κενώσει.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:16} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-16 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-16}

			For though I preach the gospel, I have nothing to glory of: for necessity is laid upon me; yea, woe is unto me, if I preach not the gospel!

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν γὰρ εὐαγγελίζωμαι, οὐκ ἔστιν μοι καύχημα, ἀνάγκη γάρ μοι ἐπίκειται· οὐαὶ γάρ μοί ἐστιν ἐὰν μὴ εὐαγγελίσωμαι.}

					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:17} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-17 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-17}

			For if I do this thing willingly, I have a reward: but if against my will, a dispensation of the gospel is committed unto me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ γὰρ ἑκὼν τοῦτο πράσσω, μισθὸν ἔχω· εἰ δὲ ἄκων, οἰκονομίαν πεπίστευμαι.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:18} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-18 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-18}

			What is my reward then?  Verily that, when I preach the gospel, I may make the gospel of Christ without charge, that I abuse not my power in the gospel.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τίς οὖν μού ἐστιν ὁ μισθός; ἵνα εὐαγγελιζόμενος ἀδάπανον θήσω τὸ εὐαγγέλιον, εἰς τὸ μὴ καταχρήσασθαι τῇ ἐξουσίᾳ μου ἐν τῷ εὐαγγελίῳ.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:19} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-19 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-19}

			For though I be free from all men, yet have I made myself servant unto all, that I might gain the more.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐλεύθερος γὰρ ὢν ἐκ πάντων πᾶσιν ἐμαυτὸν ἐδούλωσα, ἵνα τοὺς πλείονας κερδήσω·}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:20} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-20 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-20}

			And unto the Jews I became as a Jew, that I might gain the Jews; to them that are under the law, as under the law, that I might gain them that are under the law;

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἐγενόμην τοῖς Ἰουδαίοις ὡς Ἰουδαῖος, ἵνα Ἰουδαίους κερδήσω· τοῖς ὑπὸ νόμον ὡς ὑπὸ νόμον, μὴ ὢν αὐτὸς ὑπὸ νόμον, ἵνα τοὺς ὑπὸ νόμον κερδήσω·}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-21}

			To them that are without law, as without law, (being not without law to God, but under the law to Christ,) that I might gain them that are without law.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τοῖς ἀνόμοις ὡς ἄνομος, μὴ ὢν ἄνομος θεοῦ ἀλλ᾽ ἔννομος Χριστοῦ, ἵνα κερδάνω τοὺς ἀνόμους·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:22} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-22 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-22}

			To the weak became I as weak, that I might gain the weak: I am made all things to all men, that I might by all means save some.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγενόμην τοῖς ἀσθενέσιν ἀσθενής, ἵνα τοὺς ἀσθενεῖς κερδήσω· τοῖς πᾶσιν γέγονα πάντα, ἵνα πάντως τινὰς σώσω.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:23} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-23 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-23}

			And this I do for the gospel's sake, that I might be partaker thereof with you.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πάντα δὲ ποιῶ διὰ τὸ εὐαγγέλιον, ἵνα συγκοινωνὸς αὐτοῦ γένωμαι.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:24} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-24 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-24}

			Know ye not that they which run in a race run all, but one receiveth the prize?  So run, that ye may obtain.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὐκ οἴδατε ὅτι οἱ ἐν σταδίῳ τρέχοντες πάντες μὲν τρέχουσιν, εἷς δὲ λαμβάνει τὸ βραβεῖον; οὕτως τρέχετε ἵνα καταλάβητε.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:25} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-25 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-25}

			And every man that striveth for the mastery is temperate in all things.  Now they do it to obtain a corruptible crown; but we an incorruptible.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πᾶς δὲ ὁ ἀγωνιζόμενος πάντα ἐγκρατεύεται,}\footnotemark \gr{ἐκεῖνοι μὲν οὖν ἵνα φθαρτὸν στέφανον λάβωσιν, ἡμεῖς δὲ ἄφθαρτον.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}
				\footnotetext{%
					% \label{}%
					\label{EGKRATEUOMAI}
					\gr{ἐγκρατεύομαι}; BDAG 243a, ``\textbf{to keep one's emotions, impulses, or desires under control, \textit{control oneself, abstain}}.'' This word occurs twice in the NT---here and then at \hyperref[1CORINTHIANS-7-9]{1 Corinthians 7:9}.
					}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 9:26} % *1CORINTHIANS-9-26 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-9-26}

			I therefore so run, not as uncertainly; so fight I, not as one that beateth the air:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ τοίνυν οὕτως τρέχω ὡς οὐκ ἀδήλως, οὕτως πυκτεύω ὡς οὐκ ἀέρα δέρων·}

					% \item[BSV]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---

			But I keep under my body, and bring it into subjection: lest that by any means, when I have preached to others, I myself should be a castaway.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλὰ ὑπωπιάζω μου τὸ σῶμα καὶ δουλαγωγῶ, μή πως ἄλλοις κηρύξας αὐτὸς ἀδόκιμος γένωμαι.}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{1 Corinthians 10} % *1CORINTHIANS-10 
	% -----
		\label{1CORINTHIANS-10}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:1} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-1 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-1}

			MOREOVER, brethren, I would not that ye should be ignorant, how that all our fathers were under the cloud, and all passed through the sea;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὐ θέλω γὰρ ὑμᾶς ἀγνοεῖν, ἀδελφοί, ὅτι οἱ πατέρες ἡμῶν πάντες ὑπὸ τὴν νεφέλην ἦσαν καὶ πάντες διὰ τῆς θαλάσσης διῆλθον,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:2} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-2 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-2}

			And were all baptized unto Moses in the cloud and in the sea;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ πάντες εἰς τὸν Μωϋσῆν ἐβαπτίσαντο ἐν τῇ νεφέλῃ καὶ ἐν τῇ θαλάσσῃ,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:3} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-3 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-3}

			And did all eat the same spiritual meat;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ πάντες τὸ αὐτὸ πνευματικὸν βρῶμα ἔφαγον}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:4} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-4 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-4}

			And did all drink the same spiritual drink: for they drank of that spiritual Rock that followed them: and that Rock was Christ.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ πάντες τὸ αὐτὸ πνευματικὸν ἔπιον πόμα, ἔπινον γὰρ ἐκ πνευματικῆς ἀκολουθούσης πέτρας, ἡ πέτρα δὲ ἦν ὁ Χριστός·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:5} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-5 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-5}

			But with many of them God was not well pleased: for they were overthrown in the wilderness.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ οὐκ ἐν τοῖς πλείοσιν αὐτῶν ηὐδόκησεν ὁ θεός, κατεστρώθησαν γὰρ ἐν τῇ ἐρήμῳ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:6} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-6 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-6}

			Now these things were our examples, to the intent we should not lust after evil things, as they also lusted.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ταῦτα δὲ τύποι ἡμῶν ἐγενήθησαν, εἰς τὸ μὴ εἶναι ἡμᾶς ἐπιθυμητὰς κακῶν, καθὼς κἀκεῖνοι ἐπεθύμησαν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:7} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-7 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-7}

			Neither be ye idolaters, as were some of them; as it is written, The people sat down to eat and drink, and rose up to play.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μηδὲ εἰδωλολάτραι γίνεσθε, καθώς τινες αὐτῶν· ὥσπερ γέγραπται· Ἐκάθισεν ὁ λαὸς φαγεῖν καὶ πεῖν, καὶ ἀνέστησαν παίζειν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:8} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-8 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-8}

			Neither let us commit fornication, as some of them committed, and fell in one day three and twenty thousand.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μηδὲ πορνεύωμεν, καθώς τινες αὐτῶν ἐπόρνευσαν, καὶ ἔπεσαν μιᾷ ἡμέρᾳ εἴκοσι τρεῖς χιλιάδες.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:9} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-9 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-9}

			Neither let us tempt Christ, as some of them also tempted, and were destroyed of serpents.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μηδὲ ἐκπειράζωμεν τὸν Χριστόν, καθώς τινες αὐτῶν ἐπείρασαν, καὶ ὑπὸ τῶν ὄφεων ἀπώλλυντο.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:10} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-10 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-10}

			Neither murmur ye, as some of them also murmured, and were destroyed of the destroyer.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μηδὲ γογγύζετε, καθάπερ τινὲς αὐτῶν ἐγόγγυσαν, καὶ ἀπώλοντο ὑπὸ τοῦ ὀλοθρευτοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:13} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-13 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-13}

			There hath no temptation taken you but such as is common to man: but God is faithful, who will not suffer you to be tempted above that ye are able;%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					\hyperref[ALMA-13-28]{Alma 13:28} seems to clarify the scope of this idea.
					}
			but will with the temptation also make a way to escape, that ye may be able to bear it.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC64-20]{DC 64:20} for a specific example of this idea. 
					}

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πειρασμὸς ὑμᾶς οὐκ εἴληφεν εἰ μὴ ἀνθρώπινος· πιστὸς δὲ ὁ θεός, ὃς οὐκ ἐάσει ὑμᾶς πειρασθῆναι ὑπὲρ ὃ δύνασθε, ἀλλὰ ποιήσει σὺν τῷ πειρασμῷ καὶ τὴν ἔκβασιν τοῦ δύνασθαι ὑπενεγκεῖν.}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὸ ποτήριον τῆς εὐλογίας ὃ εὐλογοῦμεν, οὐχὶ κοινωνία ἐστὶν τοῦ αἵματος τοῦ Χριστοῦ; τὸν ἄρτον ὃν κλῶμεν, οὐχὶ κοινωνία τοῦ σώματος τοῦ Χριστοῦ ἐστιν;}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-27}

			If any of them that believe not bid you to a feast, and ye be disposed to go; whatsoever is set before you, eat, asking no question for conscience sake.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἴ τις καλεῖ ὑμᾶς τῶν ἀπίστων καὶ θέλετε πορεύεσθαι, πᾶν τὸ παρατιθέμενον ὑμῖν ἐσθίετε μηδὲν ἀνακρίνοντες διὰ τὴν συνείδησιν·}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-28}

			But if any man say unto you, This is offered in sacrifice unto idols, eat not for his sake that shewed it, and for conscience sake: for the earth is the Lord's, and the fulness thereof:

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν δέ τις ὑμῖν εἴπῃ· Τοῦτο ἱερόθυτόν ἐστιν, μὴ ἐσθίετε δι᾽ ἐκεῖνον τὸν μηνύσαντα καὶ τὴν συνείδησιν·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:29} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-29 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-29}

			Conscience, I say, not thine own, but of the other: for why is my liberty judged of another man's conscience?

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{συνείδησιν δὲ λέγω οὐχὶ τὴν ἑαυτοῦ ἀλλὰ τὴν τοῦ ἑτέρου· ἱνατί γὰρ ἡ ἐλευθερία μου κρίνεται ὑπὸ ἄλλης συνειδήσεως;}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 10:33} % *1CORINTHIANS-10-33 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-10-33}

			Even as I please all men in all things, not seeking mine own profit, but the profit of many, that they may be saved.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καθὼς κἀγὼ πάντα πᾶσιν ἀρέσκω, μὴ ζητῶν τὸ ἐμαυτοῦ σύμφορον ἀλλὰ τὸ τῶν πολλῶν, ἵνα σωθῶσιν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{1 Corinthians 11}
	% -----
		\label{1CORINTHIANS-11}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:1} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-1 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-1}

			BE ye followers of me, even as I also am of Christ.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μιμηταί μου γίνεσθε, καθὼς κἀγὼ Χριστοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:2} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-2 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-2}

			Now I praise you, brethren, that ye remember me in all things, and keep the ordinances, as I delivered them to you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐπαινῶ δὲ ὑμᾶς ὅτι πάντα μου μέμνησθε καὶ καθὼς παρέδωκα ὑμῖν τὰς παραδόσεις κατέχετε.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:3} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-3 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-3}

			But I would have you know, that the head of every man is Christ; and the head of the woman is the man; and the head of Christ is God.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{θέλω δὲ ὑμᾶς εἰδέναι ὅτι παντὸς ἀνδρὸς ἡ κεφαλὴ ὁ Χριστός ἐστιν, κεφαλὴ δὲ γυναικὸς ὁ ἀνήρ, κεφαλὴ δὲ τοῦ Χριστοῦ ὁ θεός.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:4} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-4 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-4}

			Every man praying or prophesying, having his head covered, dishonoureth his head.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πᾶς ἀνὴρ προσευχόμενος ἢ προφητεύων κατὰ κεφαλῆς ἔχων καταισχύνει τὴν κεφαλὴν αὐτοῦ·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:5} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-5 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-5}

			But every woman that prayeth or prophesieth with her head uncovered dishonoureth her head: for that is even all one as if she were shaven.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πᾶσα δὲ γυνὴ προσευχομένη ἢ προφητεύουσα ἀκατακαλύπτῳ τῇ κεφαλῇ καταισχύνει τὴν κεφαλὴν αὐτῆς, ἓν γάρ ἐστιν καὶ τὸ αὐτὸ τῇ ἐξυρημένῃ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:6} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-6 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-6}

			For if the woman be not covered, let her also be shorn: but if it be a shame for a woman to be shorn or shaven, let her be covered. 

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ γὰρ οὐ κατακαλύπτεται γυνή, καὶ κειράσθω· εἰ δὲ αἰσχρὸν γυναικὶ τὸ κείρασθαι ἢ ξυρᾶσθαι, κατακαλυπτέσθω.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:7} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-7 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-7}

			For a man indeed ought not to cover his head, forasmuch as he is the image and glory of God: but the woman is the glory of the man.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀνὴρ μὲν γὰρ οὐκ ὀφείλει κατακαλύπτεσθαι τὴν κεφαλήν, εἰκὼν καὶ δόξα θεοῦ ὑπάρχων· ἡ γυνὴ δὲ δόξα ἀνδρός ἐστιν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:8} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-8 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-8}

			For the man is not of the woman; but the woman of the man.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ γάρ ἐστιν ἀνὴρ ἐκ γυναικός, ἀλλὰ γυνὴ ἐξ ἀνδρός·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:9} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-9 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-9}

			Neither was the man created for the woman; but the woman for the man.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ γὰρ οὐκ ἐκτίσθη ἀνὴρ διὰ τὴν γυναῖκα, ἀλλὰ γυνὴ διὰ τὸν ἄνδρα.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:10} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-10 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-10}

			For this cause ought the woman to have power%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					= \gr{ἐξουσίαν}; see \hyperref[EXOUSIA]{\grl{ἐξουσία}}, especially the last entry in BDAG, which discusses this verse specifically.
					}
			on her head because of the angels.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{διὰ τοῦτο ὀφείλει ἡ γυνὴ ἐξουσίαν ἔχειν ἐπὶ τῆς κεφαλῆς διὰ τοὺς ἀγγέλους.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:11} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-11 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-11}

			Nevertheless neither is the man without the woman, neither the woman without the man, in the Lord.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πλὴν οὔτε γυνὴ χωρὶς ἀνδρὸς οὔτε ἀνὴρ χωρὶς γυναικὸς ἐν κυρίῳ·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:12} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-12 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-12}

			For as the woman is of the man, even so is the man also by the woman; but all things of God.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὥσπερ γὰρ ἡ γυνὴ ἐκ τοῦ ἀνδρός, οὕτως καὶ ὁ ἀνὴρ διὰ τῆς γυναικός· τὰ δὲ πάντα ἐκ τοῦ θεοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:13} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-13 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-13}

			Judge in yourselves: is it comely that a woman pray unto God uncovered?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν ὑμῖν αὐτοῖς κρίνατε· πρέπον ἐστὶν γυναῖκα ἀκατακάλυπτον τῷ θεῷ προσεύχεσθαι;}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:14} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-14 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-14}

			Doth not even nature itself teach you, that, if a man have long hair, it is a shame unto him?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐδὲ ἡ φύσις αὐτὴ διδάσκει ὑμᾶς ὅτι ἀνὴρ μὲν ἐὰν κομᾷ, ἀτιμία αὐτῷ ἐστιν,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:15} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-15 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-15}

			But if a woman have long hair, it is a glory to her: for her hair is given her for a covering.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{γυνὴ δὲ ἐὰν κομᾷ, δόξα αὐτῇ ἐστιν; ὅτι ἡ κόμη ἀντὶ περιβολαίου δέδοται.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:16} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-16 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-16}

			But if any man seem to be contentious, we have no such custom, neither the churches of God.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δέ τις δοκεῖ φιλόνεικος εἶναι, ἡμεῖς τοιαύτην συνήθειαν οὐκ ἔχομεν, οὐδὲ αἱ ἐκκλησίαι τοῦ θεοῦ.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:17} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-17 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-17}

			Now in this that I declare unto you I praise you not, that ye come together not for the better, but for the worse.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τοῦτο δὲ παραγγέλλων οὐκ ἐπαινῶ ὅτι οὐκ εἰς τὸ κρεῖσσον ἀλλὰ εἰς τὸ ἧσσον συνέρχεσθε.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:18} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-18 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-18}

			For first of all, when ye come together in the church, I hear that there be divisions among you; and I partly believe it.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πρῶτον μὲν γὰρ συνερχομένων ὑμῶν ἐν ἐκκλησίᾳ ἀκούω σχίσματα ἐν ὑμῖν ὑπάρχειν, καὶ μέρος τι πιστεύω.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:19} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-19 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-19}

			For there must be also heresies among you, that they which are approved may be made manifest among you.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{δεῖ γὰρ καὶ αἱρέσεις ἐν ὑμῖν εἶναι, ἵνα καὶ οἱ δόκιμοι φανεροὶ γένωνται ἐν ὑμῖν.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:20} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-20 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-20}

			When ye come together therefore into one place, this is not to eat the Lord's supper.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{συνερχομένων οὖν ὑμῶν ἐπὶ τὸ αὐτὸ οὐκ ἔστιν κυριακὸν δεῖπνον φαγεῖν,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:21} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-21 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-21}

			For in eating every one taketh before other his own supper: and one is hungry, and another is drunken.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἕκαστος γὰρ τὸ ἴδιον δεῖπνον προλαμβάνει ἐν τῷ φαγεῖν, καὶ ὃς μὲν πεινᾷ, ὃς δὲ μεθύει.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:22} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-22 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-22}

			What?  have ye not houses to eat and to drink in?  or despise ye the church of God, and shame them that have not?  What shall I say to you?  shall I praise you in this?  I praise you not.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}

		% ---

		% ---

			For I have received of the Lord that which also I delivered unto you, That the Lord Jesus the same night in which he was betrayed took bread:

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐγὼ γὰρ παρέλαβον ἀπὸ τοῦ κυρίου, ὃ καὶ παρέδωκα ὑμῖν, ὅτι ὁ κύριος Ἰησοῦς ἐν τῇ νυκτὶ ᾗ παρεδίδετο ἔλαβεν ἄρτον}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-24}

			And when he had given thanks, he brake it, and said, Take, eat: this is my body, which is broken for you: this do in remembrance of me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ εὐχαριστήσας ἔκλασεν καὶ εἶπεν· Τοῦτό μού ἐστιν τὸ σῶμα τὸ ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν· τοῦτο ποιεῖτε εἰς τὴν ἐμὴν ἀνάμνησιν.}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-25}

			After the same manner also he took the cup, when he had supped, saying, This cup is the new testament in my blood: this do ye, as oft as ye drink it, in remembrance of me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὡσαύτως καὶ τὸ ποτήριον μετὰ τὸ δειπνῆσαι, λέγων· Τοῦτο τὸ ποτήριον ἡ καινὴ διαθήκη ἐστὶν ἐν τῷ ἐμῷ αἵματι· τοῦτο ποιεῖτε, ὁσάκις ἐὰν πίνητε, εἰς τὴν ἐμὴν ἀνάμνησιν.}

		% ---

			For as often as ye eat this bread, and drink this cup, ye do shew the Lord's death till he come.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁσάκις γὰρ ἐὰν ἐσθίητε τὸν ἄρτον τοῦτον καὶ τὸ ποτήριον πίνητε, τὸν θάνατον τοῦ κυρίου καταγγέλλετε, ἄχρι οὗ ἔλθῃ.}

		% ---

			Wherefore whosoever shall eat this bread, and drink this cup of the Lord, unworthily, shall be guilty of the body and blood of the Lord.

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὥστε ὃς ἂν ἐσθίῃ τὸν ἄρτον ἢ πίνῃ τὸ ποτήριον τοῦ κυρίου ἀναξίως, ἔνοχος ἔσται τοῦ σώματος καὶ τοῦ αἵματος τοῦ κυρίου.}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-28}

			But let a man examine himself, and so let him eat of that bread, and drink of that cup.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{δοκιμαζέτω δὲ ἄνθρωπος ἑαυτόν, καὶ οὕτως ἐκ τοῦ ἄρτου ἐσθιέτω καὶ ἐκ τοῦ ποτηρίου πινέτω·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 11:29} % *1CORINTHIANS-11-29 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-11-29}

			For he that eateth and drinketh unworthily, eateth and drinketh \hyperref[DAMNATION]{damnation} to himself, not discerning the Lord's body.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁ γὰρ ἐσθίων καὶ πίνων} \hyperref[KRIMA]{\grl{κρίμα}} \gr{ἑαυτῷ ἐσθίει καὶ πίνει μὴ διακρίνων τὸ σῶμα.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
					\item[ASV] For he that eateth and drinketh, eateth and drinketh judgment unto himself, if he discern not the body.
					\item[NASB] For he who eats and drinks, eats and drinks judgment to himself if he does not judge the body rightly.
					\item[NIV] For those who eat and drink without discerning the body of Christ eat and drink judgment on themselves.
					\item[NRSV] For all who eat and drink without discerning the body, eat and drink judgment against themselves.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

			Wherefore, my brethren, when ye come together to eat, tarry one for another.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{Περὶ δὲ τῶν πνευματικῶν, ἀδελφοί, οὐ θέλω ὑμᾶς ἀγνοεῖν.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-12-2}

			Ye know that ye were Gentiles, carried away unto these dumb idols, even as ye were led.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οἴδατε ὅτι ὅτε ἔθνη ἦτε πρὸς τὰ εἴδωλα τὰ ἄφωνα ὡς ἂν ἤγεσθε ἀπαγόμενοι.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 12:3} % *1CORINTHIANS-12-3 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-12-3}

			Wherefore I give you to understand, that no man speaking by the Spirit of God calleth Jesus accursed: and that no man can say that Jesus is the Lord, but by the Holy Ghost.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{διὸ γνωρίζω ὑμῖν ὅτι οὐδεὶς ἐν πνεύματι θεοῦ λαλῶν λέγει· Ἀνάθεμα Ἰησοῦς, καὶ οὐδεὶς δύναται εἰπεῖν· Κύριος Ἰησοῦς εἰ μὴ ἐν πνεύματι ἁγίῳ.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

			Wherefore I give you to understand, that no man speaking by the Spirit of God calleth Jesus accursed: and that no man can say that Jesus is the Lord, but by the Holy Ghost.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Διαιρέσεις δὲ χαρισμάτων εἰσίν, τὸ δὲ αὐτὸ πνεῦμα·}

			And there are differences of administrations, but the same Lord.

		% ---

			And there are diversities of operations, but it is the same God which worketh all in all.

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ διαιρέσεις ἐνεργημάτων εἰσίν, ὁ δὲ αὐτὸς θεός, ὁ ἐνεργῶν τὰ πάντα ἐν πᾶσιν.}

		% ---

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἑκάστῳ δὲ δίδοται ἡ φανέρωσις τοῦ πνεύματος πρὸς τὸ συμφέρον.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-12-8}

			For to one is given by the Spirit the word of wisdom; to another the word of knowledge by the same Spirit;

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ᾧ μὲν γὰρ διὰ τοῦ πνεύματος δίδοται λόγος σοφίας, ἄλλῳ δὲ λόγος γνώσεως κατὰ τὸ αὐτὸ πνεῦμα,}

			To another faith by the same Spirit; to another the gifts of healing by the same Spirit;

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἑτέρῳ πίστις ἐν τῷ αὐτῷ πνεύματι, ἄλλῳ χαρίσματα ἰαμάτων ἐν τῷ ἑνὶ πνεύματι,}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 12:10} % *1CORINTHIANS-12-10 
		% ---

			For as the body is one, and hath many members, and all the members of that one body, being many, are one body: so also is Christ.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{Καθάπερ γὰρ τὸ σῶμα ἕν ἐστιν καὶ μέλη πολλὰ ἔχει, πάντα δὲ τὰ μέλη τοῦ σώματος πολλὰ ὄντα ἕν ἐστιν σῶμα, οὕτως καὶ ὁ Χριστός·}

			For by one Spirit are we all baptized into one body, whether we be Jews or Gentiles, whether we be bond or free; and have been all made to drink into one Spirit.

				\scriptsize

					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ γὰρ ἐν ἑνὶ πνεύματι ἡμεῖς πάντες εἰς ἓν σῶμα ἐβαπτίσθημεν, εἴτε Ἰουδαῖοι εἴτε Ἕλληνες, εἴτε δοῦλοι εἴτε ἐλεύθεροι, καὶ πάντες ἓν πνεῦμα ἐποτίσθημεν.}

			And those members of the body, which we think to be less honourable, upon these we bestow more abundant honour; and our uncomely parts have more abundant comeliness.

				\scriptsize

					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἃ δοκοῦμεν ἀτιμότερα εἶναι τοῦ σώματος, τούτοις τιμὴν περισσοτέραν περιτίθεμεν, καὶ τὰ ἀσχήμονα ἡμῶν εὐσχημοσύνην περισσοτέραν ἔχει,}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὰ δὲ εὐσχήμονα ἡμῶν οὐ χρείαν ἔχει. ἀλλὰ ὁ θεὸς συνεκέρασεν τὸ σῶμα, τῷ ὑστεροῦντι περισσοτέραν δοὺς τιμήν,}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-12-28}

			And God hath set some in the church, first apostles, secondarily prophets, thirdly teachers, after that miracles, then gifts of healings, helps, governments, diversities of tongues.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οὓς μὲν ἔθετο ὁ θεὸς ἐν τῇ ἐκκλησίᾳ πρῶτον ἀποστόλους, δεύτερον προφήτας, τρίτον διδασκάλους, ἔπειτα δυνάμεις, ἔπειτα χαρίσματα ἰαμάτων, ἀντιλήμψεις, κυβερνήσεις, γένη γλωσσῶν.}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{1 Corinthians 13}
	% -----
		\label{1CORINTHIANS-13}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 13:1} % *1CORINTHIANS-13-1 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-13-1}

			THOUGH I speak with the tongues of men and of angels, and have not charity, I am become as sounding brass, or a tinkling cymbal.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}

			When I was a child, I spake as a child, I understood as a child, I thought as a child: but when I became a man, I put away childish things.

			\begin{framed}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτε ἤμην νήπιος, ἐλάλουν ὡς νήπιος, ἐφρόνουν ὡς νήπιος, ἐλογιζόμην ὡς νήπιος· ὅτε γέγονα ἀνήρ, κατήργηκα τὰ τοῦ νηπίου.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 13:12} % *1CORINTHIANS-13-12 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-13-12}

			For now we see through a glass,%
				\footnote{%
					\label{1CORINTHIANS-13-12-NOTE-glass}%
					Greek \gr{ἔσοπτρον}, ``mirror.'' One of two uses of this Greek word in the NT; the other is \hyperref[JAMES-1-23]{James 1:23}. The translation ``glass'' in the KJV is short for ``looking-glass,'' which is an old way of saying ``mirror.'' 
					
					In this verse Paul is saying that we see \gr{δι᾽ ἐσόπτρου}, but that isn't even possible---you can't see through a mirror. The whole point of it is that you can't see through it becuase the light is reflected back to you so you can see yourself. So we have to think about what he might mean here. I can think of at least two things: first, when we see others, it's like looking in a mirror in that we are really seeing ourselves---what we think of others, or how we think about them, is a reflection of how we think about ourselves, and how we understand them is influenced by what is inside of us. The other way I can see this verse working is in actually trying to view an event happening around you with a mirror. You would have to angle the mirror in such a way that you could see something behind you or to your side in the mirror, but it would be hard to get a clear look at it. In addition, you cannot use a mirror in this manner to see something directly in front of you---that would be impossible (unless you had a series of mirrors, maybe). 
					
					For connections, see \hyperref[LCHRIST-055]{Christ on judging} (especially \hyperref[LCHRIST-055-MT]{Matthew 7:5}), and the idea of \hyperref[UNFOLD]{unfolding}; see also \hyperref[2CORINTHIANS-3-18]{2 Corinthians 3:18}.
					}
			darkly;%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					``Darkly'' translating \gr{ἐν αἰνίγματι}; compare \hyperref[NUMBERS-12-6]{Numbers 12:6--8}, especially verse 8, which in LXX is \gr{στόµα κατὰ στόµα λαλήσω αὐτῷ, ἐν εἴδει καὶ οὐ δι᾽ αἰνιγµάτων}---the KJV translates \gr{δι᾽ αἰνιγµάτων} as ``in dark speeches.'' 
					}
			but then face to face: now I know in part; but then shall I know even as also I am known.
			
			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{βλέπομεν γὰρ ἄρτι δι᾽ ἐσόπτρου ἐν αἰνίγματι, τότε δὲ πρόσωπον πρὸς πρόσωπον· ἄρτι γινώσκω ἐκ μέρους, τότε δὲ ἐπιγνώσομαι καθὼς καὶ ἐπεγνώσθην.}
					\item[AB 487] For at present we see by reflection, as in a mirror, but then face to face; at present I know only in part, but then I shall know fully, even as I have been fully known. 
				\end{compactdesc} 
			\end{framed}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-9}

			So likewise ye, except ye utter by the tongue words easy to be understood, how shall it be known what is spoken?  for ye shall speak into the air.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὕτως καὶ ὑμεῖς διὰ τῆς γλώσσης ἐὰν μὴ εὔσημον λόγον δῶτε, πῶς γνωσθήσεται τὸ λαλούμενον; ἔσεσθε γὰρ εἰς ἀέρα λαλοῦντες.}

		% ---

			There are, it may be, so many kinds of voices in the world, and none of them is without signification.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τοσαῦτα εἰ τύχοι γένη φωνῶν εἰσιν ἐν κόσμῳ, καὶ οὐδὲν ἄφωνον·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:11} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-11 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-11}

			Therefore if I know not the meaning of the voice, I shall be unto him that speaketh a barbarian, and he that speaketh shall be a barbarian unto me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν οὖν μὴ εἰδῶ τὴν δύναμιν τῆς φωνῆς, ἔσομαι τῷ λαλοῦντι βάρβαρος καὶ ὁ λαλῶν ἐν ἐμοὶ βάρβαρος.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-12}

			Even so ye, forasmuch as ye are zealous of spiritual gifts, seek that ye may excel to the edifying of the church.

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὕτως καὶ ὑμεῖς, ἐπεὶ ζηλωταί ἐστε πνευμάτων, πρὸς τὴν οἰκοδομὴν τῆς ἐκκλησίας ζητεῖτε ἵνα περισσεύητε.}

		% ---

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Διὸ ὁ λαλῶν γλώσσῃ προσευχέσθω ἵνα διερμηνεύῃ.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-14}

			For if I pray in an unknown tongue, my spirit prayeth, but my understanding is unfruitful.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν γὰρ προσεύχωμαι γλώσσῃ, τὸ πνεῦμά μου προσεύχεται, ὁ δὲ νοῦς μου ἄκαρπός ἐστιν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:15} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-15 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-15}

			What is it then?  I will pray with the spirit, and I will pray with the understanding also: I will sing with the spirit, and I will sing with the understanding also.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τί οὖν ἐστιν; προσεύξομαι τῷ πνεύματι, προσεύξομαι δὲ καὶ τῷ νοΐ· ψαλῶ τῷ πνεύματι, ψαλῶ δὲ καὶ τῷ νοΐ·}

					% \item[BSV]

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:16} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-16 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-16}

			Else when thou shalt bless with the spirit, how shall he that occupieth the room of the unlearned say Amen at thy giving of thanks, seeing he understandeth not what thou sayest?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐπεὶ ἐὰν εὐλογῇς πνεύματι, ὁ ἀναπληρῶν τὸν τόπον τοῦ ἰδιώτου πῶς ἐρεῖ τὸ Ἀμήν ἐπὶ τῇ σῇ εὐχαριστίᾳ; ἐπειδὴ τί λέγεις οὐκ οἶδεν·}

			For thou verily givest thanks well, but the other is not edified.

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{σὺ μὲν γὰρ καλῶς εὐχαριστεῖς, ἀλλ᾽ ὁ ἕτερος οὐκ οἰκοδομεῖται.}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-18}

			I thank my God, I speak with tongues more than ye all:

			\begin{framed}

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εὐχαριστῶ τῷ θεῷ, πάντων ὑμῶν μᾶλλον γλώσσαις λαλῶ·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:19} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-19 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-19}

			Yet in the church I had rather speak five words with my understanding, that by my voice I might teach others also, than ten thousand words in an unknown tongue.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλὰ ἐν ἐκκλησίᾳ θέλω πέντε λόγους τῷ νοΐ μου λαλῆσαι, ἵνα καὶ ἄλλους κατηχήσω, ἢ μυρίους λόγους ἐν γλώσσῃ.}

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:20} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-20 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-20}

			Brethren, be not children in understanding: howbeit in malice be ye children, but in understanding be men.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἀδελφοί, μὴ παιδία γίνεσθε ταῖς φρεσίν, ἀλλὰ τῇ κακίᾳ νηπιάζετε, ταῖς δὲ φρεσὶν τέλειοι γίνεσθε.}

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:21} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-21 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-21}

			In the law it is written, With men of other tongues and other lips will I speak unto this people; and yet for all that will they not hear me, saith the Lord.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν τῷ νόμῳ γέγραπται ὅτι Ἐν ἑτερογλώσσοις καὶ ἐν χείλεσιν ἑτέρων λαλήσω τῷ λαῷ τούτῳ, καὶ οὐδ᾽ οὕτως εἰσακούσονταί μου, λέγει κύριος.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:22} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-22 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-22}

			Wherefore tongues are for a sign, not to them that believe, but to them that believe not: but prophesying serveth not for them that believe not, but for them which believe.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὥστε αἱ γλῶσσαι εἰς σημεῖόν εἰσιν οὐ τοῖς πιστεύουσιν ἀλλὰ τοῖς ἀπίστοις, ἡ δὲ προφητεία οὐ τοῖς ἀπίστοις ἀλλὰ τοῖς πιστεύουσιν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:23} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-23 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-23}

			If therefore the whole church be come together into one place, and all speak with tongues, and there come in those that are unlearned, or unbelievers, will they not say that ye are mad?

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν οὖν συνέλθῃ ἡ ἐκκλησία ὅλη ἐπὶ τὸ αὐτὸ καὶ πάντες λαλῶσιν γλώσσαις, εἰσέλθωσιν δὲ ἰδιῶται ἢ ἄπιστοι, οὐκ ἐροῦσιν ὅτι μαίνεσθε;}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:24} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-24 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-24}

			But if all prophesy, and there come in one that believeth not, or one unlearned, he is convinced of all, he is judged of all:

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν δὲ πάντες προφητεύωσιν, εἰσέλθῃ δέ τις ἄπιστος ἢ ἰδιώτης, ἐλέγχεται ὑπὸ πάντων, ἀνακρίνεται ὑπὸ πάντων,}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:25} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-25 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-25}

			And thus are the secrets of his heart made manifest; and so falling down on his face he will worship God, and report that God is in you of a truth.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὰ κρυπτὰ τῆς καρδίας αὐτοῦ φανερὰ γίνεται, καὶ οὕτως πεσὼν ἐπὶ πρόσωπον προσκυνήσει τῷ θεῷ, ἀπαγγέλλων ὅτι Ὄντως ὁ θεὸς ἐν ὑμῖν ἐστιν.}

					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:26} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-26 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-26}

			How is it then, brethren?  when ye come together, every one of you hath a psalm, hath a doctrine, hath a tongue, hath a revelation, hath an interpretation.  Let all things be done unto edifying.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τί οὖν ἐστιν, ἀδελφοί; ὅταν συνέρχησθε, ἕκαστος ψαλμὸν ἔχει, διδαχὴν ἔχει, ἀποκάλυψιν ἔχει, γλῶσσαν ἔχει, ἑρμηνείαν ἔχει· πάντα πρὸς οἰκοδομὴν γινέσθω.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:27} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-27 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-27}

			If any man speak in an unknown tongue, let it be by two, or at the most by three, and that by course; and let one interpret.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἴτε γλώσσῃ τις λαλεῖ, κατὰ δύο ἢ τὸ πλεῖστον τρεῖς, καὶ ἀνὰ μέρος, καὶ εἷς διερμηνευέτω·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:28} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-28 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-28}

			But if there be no interpreter, let him keep silence in the church; and let him speak to himself, and to God.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν δὲ μὴ ᾖ διερμηνευτής, σιγάτω ἐν ἐκκλησίᾳ, ἑαυτῷ δὲ λαλείτω καὶ τῷ θεῷ.}

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-29}

			Let the prophets speak two or three, and let the other judge.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{προφῆται δὲ δύο ἢ τρεῖς λαλείτωσαν, καὶ οἱ ἄλλοι διακρινέτωσαν·}

		% ---

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν δὲ ἄλλῳ ἀποκαλυφθῇ καθημένῳ, ὁ πρῶτος σιγάτω.}

				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{δύνασθε γὰρ καθ᾽ ἕνα πάντες προφητεύειν, ἵνα πάντες μανθάνωσιν καὶ πάντες παρακαλῶνται}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-33}

			For God is not the author of confusion, but of peace, as in all churches of the saints.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ γάρ ἐστιν ἀκαταστασίας ὁ θεὸς ἀλλὰ εἰρήνης), ὡς ἐν πάσαις ταῖς ἐκκλησίαις τῶν ἁγίων.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:34} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-34 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-34}

			Let your women keep silence in the churches: for it is not permitted unto them to speak; but they are commanded to be under obedience, as also saith the law.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Αἱ γυναῖκες ἐν ταῖς ἐκκλησίαις σιγάτωσαν, οὐ γὰρ ἐπιτρέπεται αὐταῖς λαλεῖν· ἀλλὰ ὑποτασσέσθωσαν, καθὼς καὶ ὁ νόμος λέγει.}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-35}

			And if they will learn any thing, let them ask their husbands at home: for it is a shame for women to speak in the church.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δέ τι μαθεῖν θέλουσιν, ἐν οἴκῳ τοὺς ἰδίους ἄνδρας ἐπερωτάτωσαν, αἰσχρὸν γάρ ἐστιν γυναικὶ λαλεῖν ἐν ἐκκλησίᾳ.}

		% ---

			\begin{framed}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἢ ἀφ᾽ ὑμῶν ὁ λόγος τοῦ θεοῦ ἐξῆλθεν, ἢ εἰς ὑμᾶς μόνους κατήντησεν;}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 14:37} % *1CORINTHIANS-14-37 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-37}

			If any man think himself to be a prophet, or spiritual, let him acknowledge that the things that I write unto you are the commandments of the Lord.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Εἴ τις δοκεῖ προφήτης εἶναι ἢ πνευματικός, ἐπιγινωσκέτω ἃ γράφω ὑμῖν ὅτι κυρίου ἐστὶν·}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-14-39}

			Wherefore, brethren, covet to prophesy, and forbid not to speak with tongues.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{1 Corinthians 15} % *1CORINTHIANS-15 
	% -----
		\label{1CORINTHIANS-15}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:1} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-1 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-1}

			MOREOVER, brethren, I declare unto you the gospel which I preached unto you, which also ye have received, and wherein ye stand;

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Γνωρίζω δὲ ὑμῖν, ἀδελφοί, τὸ εὐαγγέλιον ὃ εὐηγγελισάμην ὑμῖν, ὃ καὶ παρελάβετε, ἐν ᾧ καὶ ἑστήκατε,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:2} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-2 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-2}

			By which also ye are saved, if ye keep in memory what I preached unto you, unless ye have believed in vain.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{δι᾽ οὗ καὶ σῴζεσθε, τίνι λόγῳ εὐηγγελισάμην ὑμῖν, εἰ κατέχετε, ἐκτὸς εἰ μὴ εἰκῇ ἐπιστεύσατε.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:3} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-3 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-3}

			For I delivered unto you first of all that which I also received, how that Christ died for our sins according to the scriptures;

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Παρέδωκα γὰρ ὑμῖν ἐν πρώτοις, ὃ καὶ παρέλαβον, ὅτι Χριστὸς ἀπέθανεν ὑπὲρ τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν ἡμῶν κατὰ τὰς γραφάς,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:4} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-4 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-4}

			And that he was buried, and that he rose again the third day according to the scriptures:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὅτι ἐτάφη, καὶ ὅτι ἐγήγερται τῇ ἡμέρᾳ τῇ τρίτῃ κατὰ τὰς γραφάς,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:5} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-5 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-5}

			And that he was seen of Cephas, then of the twelve:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὅτι ὤφθη Κηφᾷ, εἶτα τοῖς δώδεκα·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:6} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-6 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-6}

			After that, he was seen of above five hundred brethren at once; of whom the greater part remain unto this present, but some are fallen asleep.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔπειτα ὤφθη ἐπάνω πεντακοσίοις ἀδελφοῖς ἐφάπαξ, ἐξ ὧν οἱ πλείονες μένουσιν ἕως ἄρτι, τινὲς δὲ ἐκοιμήθησαν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:7} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-7 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-7}

			After that, he was seen of James; then of all the apostles.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔπειτα ὤφθη Ἰακώβῳ, εἶτα τοῖς ἀποστόλοις πᾶσιν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:8} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-8 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-8}

			And last of all he was seen of me also, as of one born out of due time.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἔσχατον δὲ πάντων ὡσπερεὶ τῷ ἐκτρώματι ὤφθη κἀμοί.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:9} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-9 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-9}

			For I am the least of the apostles, that am not meet to be called an apostle, because I persecuted the church of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ γάρ εἰμι ὁ ἐλάχιστος τῶν ἀποστόλων, ὃς οὐκ εἰμὶ ἱκανὸς καλεῖσθαι ἀπόστολος, διότι ἐδίωξα τὴν ἐκκλησίαν τοῦ θεοῦ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:10} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-10 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-10}

			But by the grace of God I am what I am: and his grace which was bestowed upon me was not in vain; but I laboured more abundantly than they all: yet not I, but the grace of God which was with me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{χάριτι δὲ θεοῦ εἰμι ὅ εἰμι, καὶ ἡ χάρις αὐτοῦ ἡ εἰς ἐμὲ οὐ κενὴ ἐγενήθη, ἀλλὰ περισσότερον αὐτῶν πάντων ἐκοπίασα, οὐκ ἐγὼ δὲ ἀλλὰ ἡ χάρις τοῦ θεοῦ ἡ σὺν ἐμοί.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:11} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-11 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-11}

			Therefore whether it were I or they, so we preach, and so ye believed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἴτε οὖν ἐγὼ εἴτε ἐκεῖνοι, οὕτως κηρύσσομεν καὶ οὕτως ἐπιστεύσατε.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:12} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-12 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-12}

			Now if Christ be preached that he rose from the dead, how say some among you that there is no resurrection of the dead?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Εἰ δὲ Χριστὸς κηρύσσεται ὅτι ἐκ νεκρῶν ἐγήγερται, πῶς λέγουσιν ἐν ὑμῖν τινες ὅτι ἀνάστασις νεκρῶν οὐκ ἔστιν;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:13} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-13 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-13}

			But if there be no resurrection of the dead, then is Christ not risen:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ ἀνάστασις νεκρῶν οὐκ ἔστιν, οὐδὲ Χριστὸς ἐγήγερται·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:14} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-14 
		% ---

			Yea, and we are found false witnesses of God; because we have testified of God that he raised up Christ: whom he raised not up, if so be that the dead rise not.

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εὑρισκόμεθα δὲ καὶ ψευδομάρτυρες τοῦ θεοῦ, ὅτι ἐμαρτυρήσαμεν κατὰ τοῦ θεοῦ ὅτι ἤγειρεν τὸν Χριστόν, ὃν οὐκ ἤγειρεν εἴπερ ἄρα νεκροὶ οὐκ ἐγείρονται.}

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὥσπερ γὰρ ἐν τῷ Ἀδὰμ πάντες ἀποθνῄσκουσιν, οὕτως καὶ ἐν τῷ Χριστῷ πάντες ζῳοποιηθήσονται.}

			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{δεῖ γὰρ αὐτὸν βασιλεύειν ἄχρι οὗ θῇ πάντας τοὺς ἐχθροὺς ὑπὸ τοὺς πόδας αὐτοῦ.}

			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-27}

			For he hath put all things under his feet.  But when he saith all things are put under him, it is manifest that he is excepted, which did put all things under him.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πάντα γὰρ} \hyperref[HUPOTASSO]{\grl{ὑπέταξεν}} \gr{ὑπὸ τοὺς πόδας αὐτοῦ. ὅταν δὲ εἴπῃ ὅτι πάντα} \hyperref[HUPOTASSO]{\grl{ὑποτέτακται}}\gr{, δῆλον ὅτι ἐκτὸς τοῦ} \hyperref[HUPOTASSO]{\grl{ὑποτάξαντος}} \gr{αὐτῷ τὰ πάντα.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:28} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-28 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-28}

			And when all things shall be subdued unto him, then shall the Son also himself be subject unto him that put all things under him,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The unfortunate KJV translation obscures the fact that you get three uses of the same verb here, \gr{ὑποτάσσω}.
					}
			that God may be all in all.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅταν δὲ} \hyperref[HUPOTASSO]{\grl{ὑποταγῇ}} \gr{αὐτῷ τὰ πάντα, τότε αὐτὸς ὁ υἱὸς} \hyperref[HUPOTASSO]{\grl{ὑποταγήσεται}} \gr{τῷ} \hyperref[HUPOTASSO]{\grl{ὑποτάξαντι}} \gr{αὐτῷ τὰ πάντα, ἵνα ᾖ ὁ θεὸς πάντα ἐν πᾶσιν.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-29}

			Else what shall they do which are baptized for the dead, if the dead rise not at all?  why are they then baptized for the dead?

			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐπεὶ τί ποιήσουσιν οἱ βαπτιζόμενοι ὑπὲρ τῶν νεκρῶν; εἰ ὅλως νεκροὶ οὐκ ἐγείρονται, τί καὶ βαπτίζονται ὑπὲρ αὐτῶν;}

		% ---

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τί καὶ ἡμεῖς κινδυνεύομεν πᾶσαν ὥραν;}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-31}

			I protest by your rejoicing which I have in Christ Jesus our Lord, I die daily.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καθ᾽ ἡμέραν ἀποθνῄσκω, νὴ τὴν ὑμετέραν καύχησιν, ἣν ἔχω ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ τῷ κυρίῳ ἡμῶν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:32} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-32 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-32}

			If after the manner of men I have fought with beasts at Ephesus, what advantageth it me, if the dead rise not?  let us eat and drink; for to morrow we die.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ κατὰ ἄνθρωπον ἐθηριομάχησα ἐν Ἐφέσῳ, τί μοι τὸ ὄφελος; εἰ νεκροὶ οὐκ ἐγείρονται, Φάγωμεν καὶ πίωμεν, αὔριον γὰρ ἀποθνῄσκομεν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:33} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-33 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-33}

			Be not deceived: evil communications corrupt good manners.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μὴ πλανᾶσθε· φθείρουσιν ἤθη χρηστὰ ὁμιλίαι κακαί.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-34}

			Awake to righteousness, and sin not; for some have not the knowledge of God: I speak this to your shame.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐκνήψατε δικαίως καὶ μὴ ἁμαρτάνετε, ἀγνωσίαν γὰρ θεοῦ τινες ἔχουσιν· πρὸς ἐντροπὴν ὑμῖν λαλῶ.}

		% ---

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-35}

			But some man will say, How are the dead raised up?  and with what body do they come?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἀλλὰ ἐρεῖ τις· Πῶς ἐγείρονται οἱ νεκροί, ποίῳ δὲ σώματι ἔρχονται;}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-36}

			Thou fool, that which thou sowest is not quickened, except it die:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἄφρων, σὺ ὃ σπείρεις, οὐ ζῳοποιεῖται ἐὰν μὴ ἀποθάνῃ·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:37} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-37 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-37}

			And that which thou sowest, thou sowest not that body that shall be, but bare grain, it may chance of wheat, or of some other grain:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ὃ σπείρεις, οὐ τὸ σῶμα τὸ γενησόμενον σπείρεις ἀλλὰ γυμνὸν κόκκον εἰ τύχοι σίτου ἤ τινος τῶν λοιπῶν·}
