		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:38} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-38 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-38}

			But God giveth it a body as it hath pleased him, and to every seed his own body.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁ δὲ θεὸς δίδωσιν αὐτῷ σῶμα καθὼς ἠθέλησεν, καὶ ἑκάστῳ τῶν σπερμάτων ἴδιον σῶμα.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:39} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-39 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-39}

			All flesh is not the same flesh: but there is one kind of flesh of men, another flesh of beasts, another of fishes, and another of birds.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ πᾶσα σὰρξ ἡ αὐτὴ σάρξ, ἀλλὰ ἄλλη μὲν ἀνθρώπων, ἄλλη δὲ σὰρξ κτηνῶν, ἄλλη δὲ σὰρξ πτηνῶν, ἄλλη δὲ ἰχθύων.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:40} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-40 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-40}

			There are also celestial bodies, and bodies terrestrial: but the glory of the celestial is one, and the glory of the terrestrial is another.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ σώματα ἐπουράνια, καὶ σώματα ἐπίγεια· ἀλλὰ ἑτέρα μὲν ἡ τῶν ἐπουρανίων δόξα, ἑτέρα δὲ ἡ τῶν ἐπιγείων.}
					\item[AB 585] There are both heavenly bodies and earthly bodies; the splendor of the heavenly bodies is one thing; that of the earthly is another.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:41} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-41 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-41}

			There is one glory of the sun, and another glory of the moon, and another glory of the stars: for one star differeth from another star in glory.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἄλλη δόξα ἡλίου, καὶ ἄλλη δόξα σελήνης, καὶ ἄλλη δόξα ἀστέρων, ἀστὴρ γὰρ ἀστέρος διαφέρει ἐν δόξῃ.}
					\item[AB 585] There is one splendor for the sun, another for the moon, and still another for the stars. For star differs from star in splendor.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:42} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-42 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-42}

			So also is the resurrection of the dead. It%
				\footnote{%
					\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-42-it}%
					AB 594 makes clear that the subject of these two sentences is \gr{σῶμα}; see verses 43--44.
					}
			is sown%
				\footnote{%
					\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-42-sown}%
					Passive of \gr{σπείρω}; BDAG 936b (1.b.$\delta$): ``The body after burial is compared to a seed-grain, for in the resurrection it comes forth from the earth. This is the background of the contrast \gr{σπείρειν...ἐγείρειν} in 1 Corinthians 15:42--44.'' See \hyperref[ROMANS-6-5]{Romans 6:5}, where we are ``planted together in the likeness of [Christ's] death''; the connection may not exist in Greek, however. 
					}
			in corruption; it is raised in incorruption:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὕτως καὶ ἡ ἀνάστασις τῶν νεκρῶν. σπείρεται ἐν} \hyperref[PHTHORA]{\grl{φθορᾷ}}\gr{, ἐγείρεται ἐν} \hyperref[APHTHARSIA]{\grl{ἀφθαρσίᾳ}}\gr{·}
					\item[AB 591] So too it is at the resurrection of the dead. What is sown is perishable; what is raised is imperishable.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:43} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-43 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-43}

			It is sown in dishonour; it is raised in glory: it is sown in weakness; it is raised in power:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{σπείρεται ἐν ἀτιμίᾳ, ἐγείρεται ἐν δόξῃ· σπείρεται ἐν ἀσθενείᾳ, ἐγείρεται ἐν δυνάμει·}
					\item[AB 591] It is sown in dishonor; it is raised in splendor. It is sown in weakness; it is raised in power.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:44} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-44 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-44}

			It is sown a natural body; it is raised a spiritual body. There is a natural body, and there is a spiritual body.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{σπείρεται σῶμα ψυχικόν, ἐγείρεται σῶμα πνευματικόν. Εἰ ἔστιν σῶμα ψυχικόν, ἔστιν καὶ πνευματικόν.}
					\item[AB 591] An animated body is sown, a spiritual body is raised. If there is an animated body, there is also a spiritual body.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:45} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-45 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-45}

			And so it is written, The first man Adam was made a living soul; the last Adam was made a quickening spirit.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὕτως καὶ γέγραπται· Ἐγένετο ὁ πρῶτος ἄνθρωπος Ἀδὰμ εἰς ψυχὴν ζῶσαν· ὁ ἔσχατος Ἀδὰμ εἰς πνεῦμα ζῳοποιοῦν.}
					\item[AB 591] Thus it also stands written: \textit{The} first \textit{man,} Adam, \textit{became a living being}; the last Adam, a life-giving Spirit.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:46} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-46 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-46}

			Howbeit that was not first which is spiritual, but that which is natural; and afterward that which is spiritual.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ οὐ πρῶτον τὸ πνευματικὸν ἀλλὰ τὸ ψυχικόν, ἔπειτα τὸ πνευματικόν.}
					\item[AB 591] But the spiritual was not first; rather, the animated was, and thereafter the spiritual.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:47} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-47 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-47}

			The first man is of the earth, earthy: the second man is the Lord from heaven.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁ πρῶτος ἄνθρωπος ἐκ γῆς χοϊκός, ὁ δεύτερος ἄνθρωπος ἐξ οὐρανοῦ.}
					\item[AB 591] The first man was from the earth, earthly; the second man, from heaven. 
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:48} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-48 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-48}

			As is the earthy, such are they also that are earthy: and as is the heavenly, such are they also that are heavenly.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οἷος ὁ χοϊκός, τοιοῦτοι καὶ οἱ χοϊκοί, καὶ οἷος ὁ ἐπουράνιος, τοιοῦτοι καὶ οἱ ἐπουράνιοι·}
					\item[AB 591] As was the earthly one, so too are all the earthly; and as is the heavenly one, so too are all the heavenly.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:49} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-49 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-49}

			And as we have borne the image of the earthy, we shall also bear the image of the heavenly.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ καθὼς ἐφορέσαμεν τὴν εἰκόνα τοῦ χοϊκοῦ, φορέσομεν καὶ τὴν εἰκόνα τοῦ ἐπουρανίου.}
					\item[AB 591] Just as we have borne the image of the earthly one, so too shall we bear the image of the heavenly one.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:50} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-50 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-50}

			Now this I say, brethren, that flesh and blood cannot inherit the kingdom of God; neither doth corruption inherit incorruption.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τοῦτο δέ φημι, ἀδελφοί, ὅτι σὰρξ καὶ αἷμα βασιλείαν θεοῦ κληρονομῆσαι οὐ δύναται, οὐδὲ ἡ} \hyperref[PHTHORA]{\grl{φθορὰ}} \gr{τὴν} \hyperref[APHTHARSIA]{\grl{ἀφθαρσίαν}} \gr{κληρονομεῖ.}
					\item[AB 601] Now, what I mean, brothers, is that flesh and blood cannot inherit the kingdom of God; nor does the perishable inherit imperishability. 
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:51} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-51 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-51}

			Behold, I shew you a mystery; We shall not all sleep, but we shall all be changed,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἰδοὺ μυστήριον ὑμῖν λέγω· πάντες οὐ κοιμηθησόμεθα πάντες δὲ ἀλλαγησόμεθα,}
					\item[AB 601] Look, I am telling you a mystery: we shall not all fall asleep, but we shall all be changed,
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:52} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-52 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-52}

			In a moment, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trump: for the trumpet shall sound, and the dead shall be raised incorruptible, and we shall be changed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐν ἀτόμῳ, ἐν ῥιπῇ ὀφθαλμοῦ, ἐν τῇ ἐσχάτῃ σάλπιγγι· σαλπίσει γάρ, καὶ οἱ νεκροὶ ἐγερθήσονται ἄφθαρτοι, καὶ ἡμεῖς ἀλλαγησόμεθα.}
					\item[AB 601] in an instant, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trumpet. For the trumpet will sound, and the dead will be raised imperishable, and we shall all be changed. 
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:53} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-53 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-53}

			For this corruptible must put on incorruption, and this mortal must put on immortality.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{δεῖ γὰρ τὸ} \hyperref[PHTHARTOS]{\grl{φθαρτὸν}} \gr{τοῦτο ἐνδύσασθαι} \hyperref[APHTHARSIA]{\grl{ἀφθαρσίαν}} \gr{καὶ τὸ θνητὸν τοῦτο ἐνδύσασθαι ἀθανασίαν.}
					\item[AB 601] For what is perishable must don imperishability; and what is mortal, immortality.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:54} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-54 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-54}

			So when this corruptible shall have put on incorruption, and this mortal shall have put on immortality, then shall be brought to pass the saying that is written, Death is swallowed up in victory.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅταν δὲ τὸ} \hyperref[PHTHARTOS]{\grl{φθαρτὸν}} \gr{τοῦτο ἐνδύσηται} \hyperref[APHTHARSIA]{\grl{ἀφθαρσίαν}} \gr{καὶ τὸ θνητὸν τοῦτο ἐνδύσηται ἀθανασίαν, τότε γενήσεται ὁ λόγος ὁ γεγραμμένος· Κατεπόθη ὁ θάνατος εἰς νῖκος.}
					\item[AB 601] When what is perishable dons imperishability and what is mortal dons immortality, then the saying that stands written will come true:
						``\textit{Death has been swallowed up in victory.}
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:55} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-55 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-55}

			O death, where is thy sting? O grave, where is thy victory?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ποῦ σου, θάνατε, τὸ νῖκος; ποῦ σου, θάνατε, τὸ κέντρον;}
					\item[AB 601] \textit{Where, O death, is your victory?
									Where, O death, is your sting?}''
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:56} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-56 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-56}

			 The sting of death is sin;%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Compare \hyperref[ROMANS-5-21]{Romans 5:21} and \hyperref[ROMANS-6-9]{6:9}; 
					}
			 and the strength of sin is the law.%
				\footnote{%
					\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-56-law}%
					The KJV translation really obscures the meaning of this important verse. The last half should be more like ``the effectiveness of sin is the law,'' or even more clearly, ``the only reason sin has any power at all is because of the law.'' The ``law'' referred to here is the priesthood, understood as BY \hyperref[PRIESTHOOD-BY]{understood} it. Compare \hyperref[ALMA-42-17]{Alma 42:17}: ``Now how could a man repent except he should sin? How could he sin if there was no law? How could there be a law save there was a punishment?''
					}

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὸ δὲ κέντρον τοῦ θανάτου ἡ ἁμαρτία, ἡ δὲ δύναμις τῆς ἁμαρτίας ὁ νόμος·}
					\item[AB 601] The sting of death is sin, and the power of sin is the law.
					\item[SPNT] Sin is death's sting, and the Law is sin's strength.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:57} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-57 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-57}

			But thanks be to God, which giveth us the victory through our Lord Jesus Christ.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τῷ δὲ θεῷ χάρις τῷ διδόντι ἡμῖν τὸ νῖκος διὰ τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ.}
					\item[AB 601] But thanks be to God! He grants us the victory through our Lord Jesus Christ.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 15:58} % *1CORINTHIANS-15-58 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-15-58}

			Therefore, my beloved brethren, be ye stedfast, unmoveable, always abounding in the work of the Lord, forasmuch as ye know that your labour is not in vain in the Lord.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὥστε, ἀδελφοί μου ἀγαπητοί, ἑδραῖοι γίνεσθε, ἀμετακίνητοι, περισσεύοντες ἐν τῷ ἔργῳ τοῦ κυρίου πάντοτε, εἰδότες ὅτι ὁ κόπος ὑμῶν οὐκ ἔστιν κενὸς ἐν κυρίῳ.}
					\item[AB 601--602] Consequently, my dear brothers, be steadfast, unshaken, devoting yourselves at all times to the work of the Lord, knowing that in the Lord your toil is not in vain.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{1 Corinthians 16} % *1CORINTHIANS-16 
	% -----
		\label{1CORINTHIANS-16}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:1} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-1 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-1}

			NOW concerning the collection for the saints, as I have given order to the churches of Galatia, even so do ye.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Περὶ δὲ τῆς λογείας τῆς εἰς τοὺς ἁγίους, ὥσπερ διέταξα ταῖς ἐκκλησίαις τῆς Γαλατίας, οὕτως καὶ ὑμεῖς ποιήσατε.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:2} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-2 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-2}

			Upon the first day of the week let every one of you lay by him in store, as God hath prospered him, that there be no gatherings when I come.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{κατὰ μίαν σαββάτου ἕκαστος ὑμῶν παρ᾽ ἑαυτῷ τιθέτω θησαυρίζων ὅ τι ἐὰν εὐοδῶται, ἵνα μὴ ὅταν ἔλθω τότε λογεῖαι γίνωνται.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:3} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-3 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-3}

			And when I come, whomsoever ye shall approve by your letters, them will I send to bring your liberality unto Jerusalem.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅταν δὲ παραγένωμαι, οὓς ἐὰν δοκιμάσητε δι᾽ ἐπιστολῶν, τούτους πέμψω ἀπενεγκεῖν τὴν χάριν ὑμῶν εἰς Ἰερουσαλήμ·}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:4} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-4 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-4}

			And if it be meet that I go also, they shall go with me.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐὰν δὲ ἄξιον ᾖ τοῦ κἀμὲ πορεύεσθαι, σὺν ἐμοὶ πορεύσονται.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:5} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-5 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-5}

			Now I will come unto you, when I shall pass through Macedonia: for I do pass through Macedonia.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐλεύσομαι δὲ πρὸς ὑμᾶς ὅταν Μακεδονίαν διέλθω, Μακεδονίαν γὰρ διέρχομαι,}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:6} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-6 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-6}

			And it may be that I will abide, yea, and winter with you, that ye may bring me on my journey whithersoever I go.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πρὸς ὑμᾶς δὲ τυχὸν παραμενῶ ἢ καὶ παραχειμάσω, ἵνα ὑμεῖς με προπέμψητε οὗ ἐὰν πορεύωμαι.}
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[BSV]
					% \item[ASV]
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:7} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-7 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-7}

			For I will not see you now by the way; but I trust to tarry a while with you, if the Lord permit.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ θέλω γὰρ ὑμᾶς ἄρτι ἐν παρόδῳ ἰδεῖν, ἐλπίζω γὰρ χρόνον τινὰ ἐπιμεῖναι πρὸς ὑμᾶς, ἐὰν ὁ κύριος ἐπιτρέψῃ.}

		% ---

			For a great door and \hyperref[EFFECTUAL]{effectual} is opened unto me, and there are many adversaries.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{θύρα γάρ μοι ἀνέῳγεν μεγάλη καὶ ἐνεργής, καὶ ἀντικείμενοι πολλοί.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:10} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-10 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-10}

			Now if Timotheus come, see that he may be with you without fear: for he worketh the work of the Lord, as I also do.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἐὰν δὲ ἔλθῃ Τιμόθεος, βλέπετε ἵνα ἀφόβως γένηται πρὸς ὑμᾶς, τὸ γὰρ ἔργον κυρίου ἐργάζεται ὡς κἀγώ·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:11} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-11 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-11}

			Let no man therefore despise him: but conduct him forth in peace, that he may come unto me: for I look for him with the brethren.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μή τις οὖν αὐτὸν ἐξουθενήσῃ. προπέμψατε δὲ αὐτὸν ἐν εἰρήνῃ, ἵνα ἔλθῃ πρός με, ἐκδέχομαι γὰρ αὐτὸν μετὰ τῶν ἀδελφῶν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{1 Corinthians 16:12} % *1CORINTHIANS-16-12 
		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-12}

			As touching our brother Apollos, I greatly desired him to come unto you with the brethren: but his will was not at all to come at this time; but he will come when he shall have convenient time.

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Περὶ δὲ Ἀπολλῶ τοῦ ἀδελφοῦ, πολλὰ παρεκάλεσα αὐτὸν ἵνα ἔλθῃ πρὸς ὑμᾶς μετὰ τῶν ἀδελφῶν· καὶ πάντως οὐκ ἦν θέλημα ἵνα νῦν ἔλθῃ, ἐλεύσεται δὲ ὅταν εὐκαιρήσῃ.}

		% ---
			\label{1CORINTHIANS-16-15}

			I beseech you, brethren, (ye know the house of Stephanas, that it is the firstfruits of Achaia, and that they have addicted themselves to the ministry of the saints,)

			\begin{framed}
				\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Παρακαλῶ δὲ ὑμᾶς, ἀδελφοί· οἴδατε τὴν οἰκίαν Στεφανᾶ, ὅτι ἐστὶν ἀπαρχὴ τῆς Ἀχαΐας καὶ εἰς διακονίαν τοῖς ἁγίοις ἔταξαν ἑαυτούς·}

		% ---

		% ---

			The churches of Asia salute you.  Aquila and Priscilla salute you much in the Lord, with the church that is in their house.

					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἀσπάζονται ὑμᾶς αἱ ἐκκλησίαι τῆς Ἀσίας. ἀσπάζεται ὑμᾶς ἐν κυρίῳ πολλὰ Ἀκύλας καὶ Πρίσκα σὺν τῇ κατ᾽ οἶκον αὐτῶν ἐκκλησίᾳ.}
